#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""mutants529j.py NAME SRC DST -- #529 kill table 10 (repair 12).  Imports table 9's mutator (every earlier mutant by
name), adds the Opus sixth-closing seat's mutants from its mutator copied as run (mutants_c6_opus.py, exec'd), and this
table's own: a SILENT BYPASS at each of the twelve cells repair 12's value rows carry (the flagship ruling, part A, RA1
(ii): "from the enumeration, not from the six mutants that happened to be built"), and the d->warned-keyed accept
shrink in both forms (RA3).  Every find-text occurs EXACTLY ONCE."""
import io, os, sys

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
for t in ("killtable9_20260914", "killtable8_20260914", "killtable7_20260913", "killtable6_20260913",
          "killtable5_20260913", "killtable4_20260913", "killtable3_20260913", "killtable2_20260913"):
    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(HERE, "..", t))
import mutants529i as I                                    # noqa: E402

M = dict(I.M)
_ns = {}
exec(io.open(os.path.join(HERE, "mutants_c6_opus.py"), encoding="utf-8").read().split("if __name__")[0], _ns)
M.update({k: v for k, v in _ns["M"].items() if k != "base"})

LEN8 = "\tif (len == 8) {\n"


def bypass(cond):
    """a SILENT bypass inserted before the width test: the cell skips to the common tail with no announce"""
    return [(LEN8, "\tif (%s)\n\t\tgoto crime_declined;\n\n" % cond + LEN8)]


W, R_ = "writeflag == MEM_WRITE", "writeflag == MEM_READ"
M.update({
    "S_revPsw": bypass("len == 4 && %s && relative_addr == CRIME_REV" % W),
    "S_revPsd": bypass("len == 8 && %s && relative_addr == CRIME_REV" % W),
    "S_revPld": bypass("len == 8 && %s && relative_addr == CRIME_REV" % R_),
    "S_ctlld": bypass("len == 8 && %s && relative_addr == CRIME_CONTROL" % R_),
    "S_ctlsd": bypass("len == 8 && %s && relative_addr == CRIME_CONTROL" % W),
    "S_istld": bypass("len == 8 && %s && relative_addr == CRIME_INTSTAT" % R_),
    "S_istsd": bypass("len == 8 && %s && relative_addr == CRIME_INTSTAT" % W),
    "S_istsw": bypass("len == 4 && %s && relative_addr == CRIME_INTSTAT" % W),
    "S_imsw": bypass("len == 4 && %s && relative_addr == CRIME_INTMASK" % W),
    "S_timesd": bypass("len == 8 && %s && relative_addr == CRIME_TIME" % W),
    "S_40sw": bypass("len == 4 && %s && relative_addr == 0x40" % W),
    "S_40lw": bypass("len == 4 && %s && relative_addr == 0x40" % R_),
    #  RA3: the accept guard grown by a conjunct on the LATCH -- once any refusal has set CRIME_W_LEN, an 8-byte read
    #  (W_8r) or write (W_8w) falls to the decline arm, which is silent because the latch is set
    "W_8r": [(LEN8, "\tif (len == 8 && !(%s && (d->warned & CRIME_W_LEN))) {\n" % R_)],
    "W_8w": [(LEN8, "\tif (len == 8 && !(%s && (d->warned & CRIME_W_LEN))) {\n" % W)],
})

if __name__ == "__main__":
    if sys.argv[1:2] == ["list"]:
        print(" ".join(M)); sys.exit(0)
    name, src, dst = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]
    s = io.open(src, newline="").read()
    for find, repl in M[name]:
        n = s.count(find)
        if n != 1:
            sys.stderr.write("MUTANT %s: find-text occurs %d times (want 1)\n" % (name, n)); sys.exit(2)
        s = s.replace(find, repl, 1)
    io.open(dst, "w", newline="").write(s)
    print("APPLIED %s (%d edit(s))" % (name, len(M[name])))

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""check_pred.py -- compare kill table 10's measured red-row sets (logs/lane*.out) with PRETABLE 10's predictions,
transcribed here from PRETABLE.out (written before the table).  Prints one line per run and a tally."""
import io, os, re

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
S = lambda s: frozenset(x.strip() for x in s.split(",") if x.strip())
LA, RA = "latch x5", "rearm once"
NEW = {
    "base": "",
    "c2_pre_rev": LA + ", latch said",
    "c3_pre_ctl0": LA + ", latch said",
    "c3_pre_dog": LA + ", latch said",
    "c3_pre_dogclr": LA + ", latch said",
    "c3_pre_istat": LA + ", latch said",
    "c3_pre_revbr": LA + ", latch said",
    "d2_pre_dog0200": LA + ", latch said",
    "d4_pre_wistat": LA + ", latch said",
    "o_ctl_collide": LA + ", latch said",
    "r_pre_ctlrd": LA + ", latch said",
    "r_pre_dogbr": LA + ", latch said",
    "r_pre_istat": LA + ", latch said",
    "r_pre_revbr": LA + ", latch said",
    "x_pre_dog4w": LA + ", latch said",
    "x_pre_rev_wp": LA + ", latch said",
    "x_pre_time4r": LA + ", latch said",
    "r_byp_rev": LA,
    "C1_ctl4r": "latch said",
    "C2_chan_win": "free revP said",
    "G1_acc8_ctl": "latch said, acc ctl ld, acc ctl sd",
    "G2_acc8_istat": "latch said, acc istat ld, istw sd",
    "G3_acc4_time": "latch said",
    "G4_acc4w_rev": "latch said, revP sw pc",
    "G5_acc4w_imask": "latch said, acc imask sw",
    "G6_skip8_ctl": "acc ctl ld, acc ctl sd",
    "S_revPsw": "revP sw pc", "S_revPsd": "revP sd pc", "S_revPld": "acc8 near, revP ld pc",
    "S_ctlld": "acc ctl ld", "S_ctlsd": "acc ctl sd", "S_istld": "acc istat ld", "S_istsd": "istw sd",
    "S_istsw": "istw sw", "S_imsw": "acc imask sw", "S_timesd": "acc time sd", "S_40sw": "acc 40 lw, acc 40 sw",
    "S_40lw": "acc 40 lw",
    "W_8r": "sb keeps, sh keeps, lo keeps, hi keeps, mid keeps, imask keeps, top8 st+ld, top4 w, time lb ctl, "
            "time sb ctl, time lh ctl, time sh ctl",
    "W_8w": "lo keeps",
}
CTL_FAIL = {"W_8r"}                 # predicted CRIMEP_CONTROL=FAIL (its four time ctl rows are CONTROL rows)
N_NEW = 157

got = {}
for f in ("lane0.out", "lane1.out", "lane2.out", "lane3.out"):
    p = os.path.join(HERE, "logs", f)
    if not os.path.exists(p):
        continue
    for ln in io.open(p, encoding="utf-8"):
        m = re.match(r"NEW (\S+) CRIMEP_RESULT=(\d+)/(\d+) CRIMEP_CONTROL=(\w+) (\d+) FAILING:(.*)$", ln.strip())
        if m:
            got[m.group(1)] = (int(m.group(2)), int(m.group(3)), m.group(4), int(m.group(5)), S(m.group(6)))
ok = bad = 0
for mname, pred in NEW.items():
    if mname not in got:
        print("PENDING  %s" % mname)
        continue
    a, b, ctl, done, reds = got[mname]
    want = S(pred)
    good = (reds == want and b == N_NEW and ctl == ("FAIL" if mname in CTL_FAIL else "OK") and done == 1
            and a == N_NEW - len(want))
    if good:
        ok += 1
        print("EXACT    %-18s %d/%d OK" % (mname, a, b))
    else:
        bad += 1
        print("DIFFERS  %-18s %d/%d %s done=%d  extra=%s missing=%s" % (mname, a, b, ctl, done,
              sorted(reds - want), sorted(want - reds)))
print("CHECKPRED exact=%d differs=%d of %d measured (%d predicted)" % (ok, bad, ok + bad, len(NEW)))

  46: Every red row above is exactly the row I predicted before the run. The only red rows are appended rows: no
  47: shipped row moved in any augmented leg, and base is 149/149.
  48: 
  49: ## Q1 -- VERIFY THE ENUMERATION
  50: 
  51: **Derivation from the function's own branches.** An accepted access (len 4 or 8) passes two tests in the width arm:
  52: `writeflag`, and for 4-byte accesses `relative_addr & 4`. That gives six kinds: 8W, 8R, 4W-hi, 4W-lo, 4R-hi, 4R-lo.
  53: It then goes through `switch (relative_addr)`. The labels are 8-aligned, so a 4-byte access AT a label is the hi
  54: lane. The lo lane is the default arm. The label bodies test the following:
  55: * REV tests the pc window.
  56: * CONTROL tests `writeflag`, and on writes it tests DOG, HARD_RESET and leftover-nonzero.
  57: * The default arm tests `writeflag`.
  58: * INTSTAT, INTMASK, DOG and TIME have no `if` in this function.
  59: 
  60: So the latch floor is:
  61: 
  62: | label | floor cells | count |
  63: |---|---|---|
  64: | REV | {8W,8R,4W,4R} x window {in,out} | 8 |
  65: | CONTROL | reads {8R,4R}, plus writes {8W,4W} x {DOG set/clear} x {leftover 0/nonzero} (HARD_RESET's true side disclosed) | 10 |
  66: | INTSTAT, INTMASK, DOG, TIME | {8W,8R,4W,4R} each | 16 |
  67: | default | 6 kinds | 6 |
  68: | **total** | | **40** |
  69: 
  70: **`ACCEPTS` (46) = these 40 + the two named ENDIANESS strengthenings + 4 positional repeats of default kinds**
  71: (`sw/lw 0x04` repeat 0x34's lo-lane kinds; `sd/ld 0x278` repeat 0x40's 8-byte kinds). No floor cell is missing for the
  72: PRE-SET/RE-ARM class, and every listed kind is takeable -- `ldP` is correctly routed by `accept()`.
  73: 
  74: **BUT THE TWO ROWS ACCEPTS FEEDS HAVE A CHANNEL THAT CANNOT VARY UNDER AN ACCEPT-SET SHRINK -- of ANY of its 46
  75: cells.** Both rows count `-- declined.`:
  76: * In `rearm once`, a shrunk cell comes after the first refusal, so its decline is silenced by the latch.
  77: * In `latch x5`, the first shrunk cell in the prologue IS the session's first refusal. It takes the one notice, the
  78:   lh +0x06 after it is silent, and the count is still 1.
  79: 
  80: So ACCEPTS carries the latch cross and carries **nothing** about whether its cells are still accepted. For a cell whose only
  81: accepted access in the probe is in those two sessions, a shrink is invisible. Per-cell, derived by grepping every
  82: accepted access outside ACCEPTS:
  83: 
  84: | ACCEPTS cell(s) | accept side pinned elsewhere? | a shrink there would... |
  85: |---|---|---|
  86: | REV 8R (`ld 0`), 4R window (`lwP`) | yes: `acc8 near`, `rev pc ctl` | -- |
  87: | REV 8W/4W/4R out of window (`sd/sw/lw 0`), `sw 0x04` | no | be value-equivalent (the prelude rewrites REV; the hi half is 0) -- notice only |  91: | CONTROL 4W (`sw 8` x5) | yes, for an address+direction shrink: `sw08 halts`, `sw08 msg ctl` | (a shrink keyed on the DOG-set value cells is not seen -- clause (e), a value joint) |
  92: | **INTSTAT 8W, 8R, 4W** | **no** | **drop the reassert / answer 0 with an irq pending (G2 measured)** |
  93: | INTSTAT 4R; INTMASK 8W, 8R, 4R | yes: `istat ctl`; `imask said`+`imask ctl`; `imask keeps`; `imask ctl`, `gate line` | -- |
  94: | **INTMASK 4W** | **no** | **not land (G5 measured)** |
  95: | DOG x4, `sw/lw 0x34` | yes: `acc8 st+ld`, `acc4 *` | -- |
  96: | TIME 8R | yes: `time * ctl` | -- |
  97: | **TIME 8W** (`sd 0x38`; the dog session's `sd 0x38` comes after its first refusal, and its refused reads answer 0 either way) | **no** | **lose the seed (reasoned, not built)** |
  98: | TIME 4W, 4R | no | be value-equivalent (hi half 0; updates accumulate) -- notice only (G3 measured) |
  99: | default `sd/ld 0x40`, `sd/ld 0x278` | yes: `lo keeps`, `top *` | -- |
 100: | **default `sw/lw 0x40`** (4-byte hi lane, interior) | **no** | **lose the value** -- the address-keyed form falls under the disclosed "unrowed slot" class |
 101: 
 102: **THE FLOOR ARGUMENT, so this is graded on the rules and not on the count.** Four points, strongest last.
 103: 1. **The de-escalation clause.** C1 and G1-G5 are exactly its shape: a growth of the predicate that used to gate
 104:    death. Pre-fix, the else-arm was `exit(1)`, so `len == 8 && relative_addr != CRIME_CONTROL` would have ended the
 105:    process at the first 64-bit CONTROL access. That is self-announcing. Post-fix, the same edit declines once and
 106:    nothing reds. The clause says those accept rows are the fix's own cost.
 107: 2. **The JOIN clause.** The label is a floor axis of that guard. `relative_addr` flows into
 108:    `d->reg[relative_addr / 8]`, which the guarded body reads and writes, and the switch re-tests it after the join.
 109: 3. **The contract's own item 6.** It claims "the ACCEPT SET shrunk at any single cell". The DISCLOSED list's
 110:    "unrowed slots" bullet names +0x08, +0x10, +0x18 and +0x38 as ROWED slots.
 111: 4. **This exact edit shape was already found once, at one label, and repaired only there.** Grok's closing-pass
 112:    `len == 8 && relative_addr != 0` is why `acc8 near` exists. The identical edit with `CRIME_CONTROL` or
 113:    `CRIME_INTSTAT` passes today. That is the #499 shape: a class repaired at the address the mutant used.
 114: 
 115: **I grade it L2, a FLOOR breach, and a class that owes CLASS CLOSURE rather than a cell.** C1 on its own is
 116: value-equivalent at the guest, so it shows only on the notice channel. G1, G2, G4 and G5 are guest-visible and
 117: leave no room for an equivalence argument.
 118: 
 119: ## THE REPAIR -- codex's row, MEASURED, and it closes the whole announced class at once
 120: 
 121: `row("latch said", said(t, 2, 0x6, clean), 1, ...)` goes in latch x5, beside `latch x5`. It can equally be folded
 122: into `latch x5` as count == 1 AND said == 1. It is red on **C1, G1, G2, G3, G4 and G5**, and green on base (149/149,
 123: no other row moved). **Why it is general:** all 46 ACCEPTS cells sit in latch x5's prologue BEFORE the lh +0x06. Any
 124: guard-growth shrink of any of them sends that cell into the announcing decline arm, where it takes the notice the lh
 125: should have had. So one row turns the prologue from a latch-only detector into an ACCEPT-SET detector for every
 126: ACCEPTS cell. No new session is needed and the command list is unchanged.
 127: 
 128: **What it cannot see: a SILENT bypass (G6, measured 148/149 with `latch said` GREEN).** I grade G6 outside the
 129: de-escalation class. The deleted `exit(1)` would not have announced a new bypass inserted before the width test
 130: either: pre-fix `if (...) return 1;` is equally silent. It is, however, inside item 6's literal "any single cell"
 131: sentence. Two dispositions are open, and the choice is the adjudicator's:
 132: * (i) narrow item 6 to "any single cell, in the guard-growth form" and DISCLOSE the silent bypass by class; or
 133: * (ii) add VALUE rows at the uncovered label cells as a licensed clause-(e) strengthening.
 134: 
 135: `dg ld ctl` / `dg ld istat` / `dg sw imask` in my diagnostic session are a measured template: one session, green on
 136: base, and each red on its cell. So is `dg swP pc`, in its own one-access session. The same holds, reasoned and not
 137: built, for a shrink keyed on `d->warned`: it is silent once the latch is set, and it is a joint on an lvalue the
 138: accept body does not read, so it is clause (e).
 139: 
 140: **Side effect the next table must carry.** A pre-set mutant also silences the lh, so every table-9 pre-set kill
 141: (the 17 that red `latch x5`) will also red `latch said`, and its score drops by one more. Re-arm kills are
 142: unchanged, because the lh's own text still appears exactly once. The targeting rule is not triggered, because no
 143: command list changed. Even so, the new row is floor material and needs its own targeted table.
 144: 
 145: ## C2 -- measured 141/141; one free-running window refusal kills it (148/149, no other row moved)
 146: 
 147: Every free-running refusal in the probe runs from `CODE`, at 0x800010xx. The window refusals (`lb/lh/sb/sh rev`,
 148: `rev once`, `rev said`) are all stepped, and a stepped probe cannot tell `debug` from `fatal`.
 149: 
 150: **Grade: clause (e) by the DEPENDS-ON bound.** The decline arm does not read `cpu->pc`, and the window predicate does
 151: not dominate it. **Recommend adding `free revP said` anyway, as a licensed strengthening.** The PROM's probe loop is
 152: the one real-world code path that issues CRIME accesses from that window, and item 3 already treats "from the
 153: window" as a refusal cell. With C2, a narrow access from the PROM would be declined in silence in a default,
 154: non-verbose run. The row is measured, cheap (one session), and moved nothing. At minimum the DISCLOSED list owes a
 155: line for it: item 5's "EVERY axis of the refusal" does not name pc, and no disclosure does either.
 156: 
 157: ## C3, C4, C5
 158: 
 159: * **C3 is killed in both forms**, pre-set and re-arm, at 139/141 (`mid said`, `mid once`). kimi-k3's premise, "no
 160:   ACCEPTS entry sets bits 7/8", is TRUE. The conclusion does not follow from it, because the mid session's accepted
 161:   `sd +0x1f8` has bits 7 and 8 set, and it runs both before the first refusal (pre-set) and between refusals (re-arm).
 162: * **C4 is killed**, 131/141 over ten answer rows. minimax's premise, "item 9 rows the answer at named labels only",
 163:   is FALSE. The default-arm answer rows sit at +0x07, +0x06, +0x27b, +0x27a, +0x1ff and +0x1fe, which are all
 164:   non-8-aligned. (The label rows read `& 7` = 0 and stay green, as expected.)
 165: * **C5 is EQUIVALENT, measured 141/141 and provable by reading.** `idata` is initialised to 0 and assigned only under
 166:   `if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE)`. `odata` is consumed only under `if (writeflag == MEM_READ)`. So on every path the
 167:   consumed `odata` is 0, exactly as in base. The binary differs from base because of codegen, not behaviour. No row
 168:   is owed; one DISCLOSED line would stop it being re-raised.
 169: 
 170: ## MY OWN HUNT -- denominator
 171: 
 172: **Built and swept on the 141-row probe (13 binaries):**
 173: 
 174: | class | mutants | result |
 175: |---|---|---|
 176: | accept-guard growth, 8-byte | CONTROL (G1), INTSTAT (G2) | escaped |
 177: | accept-guard growth, 4-byte | CONTROL R (C1), TIME (G3), REV W (G4), INTMASK W (G5) | escaped |
 178: | silent accept bypass | CONTROL 8 (G6) | escaped |
 179: | latch key on address bits 7/8 | pre-set (C3p), re-arm (C3r) | killed |
 180: | notice channel keyed on pc | C2 | escaped |

 182: 
 183: **Reasoned from the per-cell table, NOT built:**

 193: 
 194: ## Q3 -- WRONG STATEMENTS
 195: 
 196: 1. **Probe header, contract item 6: "the ACCEPT SET shrunk at any single cell"** is FALSE. C1, G1, G2, G3, G4 and G5
 197:    each scored 141/141. It holds at REV 8R/4R-window, DOG, CONTROL 4W (address+direction), INTMASK 8W/8R/4R,
 198:    INTSTAT 4R, TIME 8R and the default +0x40/+0x278 8-byte cells. It does not hold at the cells in the table above.
 199: 2. **DISCLOSED, "ACCEPT cells at unrowed slots: the rows touch accepted accesses at +0x00, +0x04, +0x08, +0x10, +0x18,
 200:    ... +0x38"** implies that a touched slot is pinned. **Touching a cell in ACCEPTS pins its latch behaviour, never its
 201:    acceptance.**
 202: 3. **DISCLOSED, "an accepted WRITE (4 or 8 bytes) at +0x00 or +0x08 shrunk away: ... no round trip there can observe
 203:    whether the store was accepted (the CONTROL arm's effects are rowed separately as sw08 halts / sw08 msg ctl)"** is
 204:    wrong twice:
 205:    * At +0x00 from the PROM window, an accepted write REDIRECTS THE PC. The REV arm ignores the direction, which is
 206:      item 3's own words, and **G4 measured it: `dg swP pc` read 0xbfc05184 against ra**.
 207:    * The CONTROL arm's effects are rowed for 4-byte writes only. An 8-byte CONTROL write shrink is unrowed (G1).
 208: 4. **Brief, "is `ACCEPTS` now exactly the (branch side x kind) floor"**: yes for the latch class (40 + 2 + 4). The
 209:    question as framed cannot see that the rows ACCEPTS feeds are blind to the ACCEPT side of the same 46 cells.
 210:    **"46 kinds" is also slightly loose**: 4 are positional repeats, not kinds.
 211: 5. **Item 5's "on EVERY axis of the refusal"** has no line for the issuing pc (C2), although items 3 and 4 treat "from
 212:    the window" as a cell.
 213: 6. **POSTTABLE 9's FLOOR line, "(d) the window x kind cross is closed"**, is true for pre-set/re-arm. Read beside
 214:    item 6, it could suggest the accept side of the same cross is closed too, and it is not.
 215: 
 216: ## What I could not check
 217: 
 218: * I did not re-run tables 1-9 against the augmented probe. The "17 pre-set kills also red `latch said`" prediction
 219:   is reasoned from the mechanism, not measured.
 220: * I did not build the four "reasoned" growths or the `d->warned` shrink.
 221: * I did not check real-O2 behaviour for 64-bit CONTROL or INTSTAT access, or which widths NetBSD and the PROM use.
 222:   The guest-visibility claims above are about the model's own answers.
 223: * Operational note: my `killtails.sh` `pkill -f 'gxopuscrimec6_work/lane_'` also matched ten of my own idle
 224:   wait-loops, which exited 15. Those were waiters, not measurements, and every measured leg had finished before it
 225:   ran.
 226: 
 227: **Clone status:** `/tmp/gxopuscrimec6`, `/tmp/gxopuscrimec6_bins` and `/tmp/gxopuscrimec6_work` are **RESTORED, then
 228: DELETED**. At deletion the source was CLEAN against SEC, the binary was newer than the source, and there was no
 229: sentinel (`finish6.sh`). I wrote nothing into GXEMUL-SEC except this directory. I did not touch `/tmp/gxopus530c4`,
 230: `/tmp/gxopuscrime` or `/tmp/gxlca530`.

import ast, pathlib, re
p = pathlib.Path('regress/crime_width_probe.py')
s = p.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
t = ast.parse(s)
frozen = ast.parse(pathlib.Path('regress/artifacts/crimewidthkill/killtable10_20260915/crime_width_probe.table10.py').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
print('Shipped/frozen executable AST identical:', ast.dump(ast.Module(body=t.body[1:], type_ignores=[])) == ast.dump(ast.Module(body=frozen.body[1:], type_ignores=[])))
brief = pathlib.Path('../_scratchpad/panel_20260915_073005/brief_sent.md').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
source = pathlib.Path('src/devices/dev_sgi_ip32.c').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
inline_function = brief.split('"'```c'"\\n',1)[1].split('\\n"'```'"',1)[0]
start = source.index('DEVICE_ACCESS(crime)\\n')
end = source.index('\\n}\\n',start)+2
print('Inlined DEVICE_ACCESS matches tree:', inline_function == source[start:end])
header = brief.split('## THE TEST\\'S HEADER',1)[1].split('"'```'"\\n',1)[1].split('\\n"'```'"',1)[0]
print('Inlined contract/disclosures match tree:', header.strip() == s[s.index('CONTRACT --'):s.index('Sentinel:')].strip())
accepts = ast.literal_eval(next(n.value for n in t.body if isinstance(n,ast.Assign) and any(isinstance(x,ast.Name) and x.id=='ACCEPTS' for x in n.targets)))
print('ACCEPTS count:',len(accepts))
for i,(op,off,val) in enumerate(accepts,1): print('%02d %-3s +0x%03x value=0x%x' %(i,op,off,val))
logroot = pathlib.Path('regress/artifacts/crimewidthkill/killtable10_20260915/logs')
logs = sorted(logroot.glob('probe_*.raw'))

"C:\\WINDOWS\\System32\\WindowsPowerShell\\v1.0\\powershell.exe" -Command "@'
import ast, pathlib, re
src = pathlib.Path('regress/crime_width_probe.py').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
tree = ast.parse(src)
# Run only command construction in memory. Replace all process interaction and row output.
kept = []
for n in tree.body:
    if n.lineno < 266 or isinstance(n, (ast.Import, ast.ImportFrom)):
        continue
    if isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef) and n.name in ('session','row'):
        continue
    kept.append(n)
sessions=[]
ns={'re':re,'BIN':'NOT-RUN','LABEL':'COMMAND-CONSTRUCTION-ONLY','print':lambda *args,**kwargs:None}
def session(cmds, **kwargs):
    sessions.append({'cmds':list(cmds),'rows':[]})
    return '',True
def row(name,got,want,note=''):
    sessions[-1]['rows'].append(name)
    ns['rows'].append((name,got==want))
ns.update(session=session,row=row)
exec(compile(ast.Module(body=kept,type_ignores=[]),'<command construction only>','exec'),ns)
opnames={v:k for k,v in ns['OPC'].items()}
regnames={v:k for k,v in ns['R'].items()}
for s in sessions:
    registers={}; words={}; events=[]; pc=0
    for c in s['cmds']:
        m=re.fullmatch(r'(\\w+)=0x([0-9a-f]+)',c)
        if m:
            if m[1]=='pc': pc=int(m[2],16)
            else: registers[m[1]]=int(m[2],16)
        m=re.fullmatch(r'put w 0x([0-9a-f]+), 0x([0-9a-f]+)',c)
        if m: words[int(m[1],16)]=int(m[2],16)
        m=re.fullmatch(r'step (\\d+)',c)
        if m:
            for i in range(int(m[1])):
                ins=words.get(pc+4*i,0); op=opnames.get(ins>>26)
                if op:
                    base=regnames[(ins>>21)&31]; rt=regnames[(ins>>16)&31]
                    address=registers.get(base,0)+(ins&65535)
                    if ns['CRIME']<=address<ns['CRIME']+0x280:
                        events.append((op,address-ns['CRIME'],registers.get(rt,0) if op.startswith('s') else 0,pc+4*i))
    s['events']=events
latch=next(s for s in sessions if 'latch said' in s['rows'])
actual=[(op+('P' if pc==ns['PROBE_PC'] else ''),off,val) for op,off,val,pc in latch['events'][:46]]
print('Sessions:',len(sessions),'rows:',sum(len(s['rows']) for s in sessions))
print('Latch first 46 decoded accesses exactly match ACCEPTS:',tuple(actual)==ns['ACCEPTS'])
print('Latch next access:',latch['events'][46])
for title,predicate in [('CONTROL DOG writes',lambda e:e[0] in ('sw','sd') and e[1]==8 and e[2]&0x200),('TIME 4-byte accesses',lambda e:e[0] in ('sw','lw') and e[1]==0x38),('default sw +0x04',lambda e:e[0]=='sw' and e[1]==4)]:
    print(title)
    for s in sessions:
        hit=[(op,hex(off),hex(val)) for op,off,val,pc in s['events'] if predicate((op,off,val,pc))]
        if hit: print('  ',hit,'observed by:',', '.join(s['rows']))

codex
The command reconstruction confirms all 46 ACCEPTS entries run before `lh +0x06`, so the announced-decline proof holds. The frozen probe also matches the shipped executable code, and the recorded 40-leg results pass their checker. The remaining problems are in the silent-cell dispositions: DOG-enabled CONTROL diagnostics are unobserved, and skipping TIME updates can affect a later read despite the “equivalent” label.
codex
ENUMERATION WRONG: TIME 4R/4W; CONTROL sw/sd 0x0201. CRIME_CLOSE7_20260915

## 1. Silent-cell dispositions need correction

### TIME 4R/4W are observable, not equivalent

Item 6’s “high half is 0; updates accumulate” argument overlooks **when a read samples TIME**. The width arm captures `odata` before the switch updates TIME. Skipping an intermediate access therefore changes what the next accepted `ld` returns. See [the read path](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_sgi_ip32.c:275) and [TIME’s switch arm](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_sgi_ip32.c:392).

For example, with TIME initially zero and no intervening device tick:

- A 4-byte access after one elapsed microsecond updates TIME to 66.
- The next `ld` returns 66.
- Bypass that 4-byte access, and the next `ld` returns zero. Its subsequent update cannot repair the value already returned.

This applies to both `lw` and `sw 0` at `+0x38`.

Exact silent mutation, inserted immediately before the width test:

```c
if (len == 4 && relative_addr == CRIME_TIME)
	goto crime_declined;
```

**Predicted 157/157; not measured here.** Command reconstruction confirms these 4-byte accesses occur only in the re-arm and latch sessions, which observe notices and survival, not TIME.

**Required correction:** classify both cells as observable, unrowed silent bypasses. The broad silent-class disclosure covers their scope, but their equivalence claim is wrong.

### CONTROL’s DOG-set, leftover-nonzero cells remain unobserved

ACCEPTS explicitly distinguishes `0x0201` from `0x0001`, at both write widths. However, `sw08 msg ctl` and `acc ctl sd` exercise only `0x01`. The `0x0201` accesses occur exclusively in sessions that do not count CONTROL diagnostics. See [ACCEPTS](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/crime_width_probe.py:770) and [the new CONTROL write](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/crime_width_probe.py:1055).

Exact mutation before the width test:

```c
if (len == 8 && relative_addr == CRIME_CONTROL && idata == 0x0201)
	goto crime_declined;
```

**Predicted 157/157; not measured here.** Changing `8` to `4` targets the corresponding `sw` cell. Each removes an observable CONTROL diagnostic without affecting the asserted rows. `acc ctl sd` cannot catch the first mutation because its input is `0x01`.

The original [Opus enumeration explicitly qualified the 4W claim](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/crimewidthkill/opus_close6_20260915/OPUS_REPORT.md:91). Repair 12’s “remaining … pinned” wording loses that qualification.

**Required correction:** disclose these two cells individually, or add diagnostic assertions covering both widths.

### `sw +0x04` also needs an explicit disposition

Its stored value is overwritten by the next access’s REV prelude, but bypassing it suppresses the default-arm write diagnostic. No row observes that diagnostic. It is **guest-value-equivalent with an unrowed diagnostic difference**, rather than “pinned elsewhere.”

## 3. The announced-form generality argument holds

I reconstructed the probe’s commands in memory and decoded the stepped accesses:

- The first 46 CRIME accesses in the latch session exactly match ACCEPTS, including all four PROM-window kinds.
- Access 47 is `lh +0x06`.
- The session starts with `warned` clear. Ordinary accepted accesses do not set it.
- A guard growth that rejects a prologue cell reaches the unchanged announcing arm before the register switch. Its `len=4` or `len=8` text cannot satisfy the `len=2 at 0x6` assertion.
- Subsequent accesses cannot clear the latch, so `latch said` fails.

There is no path through that decline arm that stays silent while its latch is clear. The construction is visible at [the prologue](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/crime_width_probe.py:977) and [the announcing arm](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_sgi_ip32.c:314).

**I found no floor gap in this scoped guard-growth class.** The silent counterexamples above expose incorrect dispositions within the separately disclosed class.

## 4. New channels and recorded evidence

The new target rows can vary under their actual table-10 mutants:

- **IP2:** `S_istsd` and `S_istsw` independently change their respective target row from `set` to `clear`; both setup controls remain clear. The common tail recomputes INTSTAT without asserting the line.
- **TIME parity:** `S_timesd` changes `odd` to `even`. Adding multiples of 66 and truncating to 32 bits preserves parity, so the odd seed distinguishes a landed store.
- **Other channels:** the REV rows observe redirect versus sequential PC; value rows observe nonzero versus zero; diagnostic rows observe one occurrence versus none. The CONTROL diagnostic proof covers the tested `0x01` input.

I verified that the inlined function and contract match the tree, the shipped and frozen probes have identical executable ASTs, all 40 raw logs terminate correctly, and the read-only prediction checker reports **40 exact, zero differences**.

Two record corrections follow:

1. [Item 6](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/crime_width_probe.py:103) and [POSTTABLE’s closure wording](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/crimewidthkill/killtable10_20260915/POSTTABLE.out:32) must distinguish the twelve measured bypass predicates from a complete disposition of all 46 entries.
2. The brief’s “MUTANTS … verbatim” section is truncated at `.split("` and omits the `S_*` and `W_*` definitions. I checked those in the actual mutator.

**Measurement limit:** I ran no emulator, build, gate, or battery; this workspace is read-only. The missing measurements are separate silent bypasses for CONTROL 4W/8W with DOG set and TIME 4R/4W. Their predicted surviving scores must remain predictions until run.
